ParlaMint-GR_2020-07-29-S1-commons.u500	Mr President, Minister, ladies and gentlemen, following the laws of the Ministry of Development and Investment and the Ministry of Rural Development, which have been passed in the last few days by this House, and through which provisions have been laid down to support labour, better functioning of the market, to strengthen entrepreneurship and to solve problems in the rural and livestock world, our government today brings us a draft law which has development content. In addition to the incorporation of directives into the internal legal system and the strengthening of tax administration, the aim of the Ministry of Finance through this bill is double. On the one hand, it supports the development process of the Greek economy through fiscal policy, which for the Government of Kyriakos Mitsotakis is a primary tool of development and not a means of bleeding taxpayers, as was the case with the previous government. On the other hand, he stands on the side of those affected by the pandemic, protecting, among other things, experts, who have received loans and with arguable insurance in the main home. As far as the development content of the bill is concerned, arrangements are first introduced to strengthen the favourable tax environment for citizens and businesses which the Government of Kyriakos Mitsotakis has established and established in the country. And since the Prime Minister has a very high priority for the agricultural and livestock population and rural development, in addition to the support he has given so far at the height of the EUR 35 million available from the state budget to ELGA for payment of debts and for ensuring stable resources for the CFP, this draft law exempts farmers and farmers from the obligation to pay a trade fee for them five years from the date of their accession to the normal VAT regime, as are coastal fishermen. Secondly, the autonomous taxation of 10% of the financial benefits and donations from parents to their children is abolished. Third, a further significant step is taking to stimulate economic activity, as the tax advance for 2019 is reduced or even zero, depending on the drop in turnover shown by enterprises this year. Fourthly, the scheme of car registration fees for the majority of vehicles is more favourable, with the aim of renewing the current fleet of cars, while a new system of progressive taxation is introduced and the hybrid car registration fee is further reduced. Fifth, it is exempted from any tax or value of inland vouches of tourism of a total value of up to EUR 300 provided by employers, while incentives are provided to pensioners who have a tax home abroad to transfer their tax home to Greece, subject to an alternative tax on foreign income at a tax rate of 7% for a period of 15 years. Sixth, 6% of music books are transferred to the excess VAT rate to promote music education, while at the same time being subject to the reduced VAT rate of 13% of sports tickets from the beginning of September 2020 until the end of June 2021. Tax policy measures reflect our Government's multi-level strategy for fair and uniform development. We promote the free economy and the smooth functioning of the market under the ultimate and regulatory role of the social state, which defends social cohesion and is next to those who need it and who need its support. In this draft law, we are energising the "GEFYRA" programme to support borrowers with a mortgage on their first residence, who have been hit by the coronary, both for service and for non-performing loans. In other words, a public contribution programme is introduced for the repayment of loans with policyholders at the main home for borrowers affected by the pandemic. Of course, we should make it clear that the affected include even those borrowers who have not been affected themselves, but are a direct member of their family, and as a result they are regarded as affected by the whole family. In addition, the bill contains provisions on decongestion of administrative justice and strengthening the work of the tax administration. In particular, the Secretariat-General for Tax Policy and Public Property shall set up a Committee on the extrajudicial resolution of pending tax disputes before the Council of State and the ordinary administrative courts in order to ensure that cases are dealt with promptly. The Committee will operate with three-member compositions consisting of two former judges and a member of the Legal Board of the State. Finally, the Government of Kyriakos Mitsotakis is now moving on to a historic political act. As of 1 August 2020, this law stipulates that the provision of a life-long monthly retirement pension of EUR 200 to Greek soldiers who participated in the events in Cyprus in the years 1964, 1967 and 1974. We, gentlemen, do not trade patriotism, we are patriots, we have fully shielded the Greek territory from Evros to Kastelorizo. After the second halt of the expansion of Ankara in the Aegean Sea, the Prime Minister honoured those who fought for Cypriot Hellenism leading the country to a time of uplifting and recovery, when Greece is strong in its region as a pillar of Euro-Atlantic integration in the New Democracy era. Of course we are voting in favour of this bill. Thank you.
